```text
Now I have the complete picture. Let me summarize:

1. **eval-pi05-reconvla-spatial-s42**: FAILED with `torch.OutOfMemoryError` on GPU 0. The process (PID 395002 per registry, but log says process 388593 was using the GPU) crashed during model loading at `Pi05Wrapper.__init__` → `PI05Policy.from_pretrained`. GPU 0 had 14.30 GiB used by process 388593 and only 26.81 MiB free. The interleaved progress messages ("pick_up_the_black_bowl...") appear to be stdout pollution from another concurrent process writing to the same terminal/log - this is garbled output.


2. **eval-pi05-reconvla-goal-s42** is running on GPU 2 with about half the memory available, having completed the first task at 90% success and currently working through the second task. Meanwhile, **eval-pi05-reconvla-object-s42** is also running on GPU 3 with similar memory usage.

3. **eval-pi05-reconvla-spatial-s42-bf16** appears to have never started—there's no log file or bf16 variant anywhere, so I need to update the registry and investigate what happened with that experiment.
```
